849:11. **Anthropic-native structured outputs over 3rd-party** — *r32 NEW*. `output_config.format` + `strict:true` + 24h grammar cache. **PREFILL DEPRECATED April 2026** on latest models.
851:12. **Context-resets > compaction** — *r33 NEW Anthropic Mar 24*. Clearing context entirely and starting fresh agent OUTPERFORMS in-place compaction for long-running tasks. Use `/clear` between sprint contracts; save state to memory file.
853:13. **State-machine workflows for resumable processes** — *r38 NEW*. Distinct from DAG. Use explicit state-machine semantics when workflow needs resume-after-crash or branch-tracking-beyond-dependencies.
855:14. **Worker-reviewer contract templates** — *r38 NEW*. Explicit contract: input-schema / output-schema / acceptance-criteria. Reduces ambiguous failures across worker-reviewer boundary.
857:15. **Operating-rules router format** — *r38 NEW*. Document tool-selection heuristics in router file — explicit decision tree (situation, tool). Easier to audit than implicit policy embedded in prompts.
859:16. **Adversarial self-critique — `grill-me` pattern** — *r48 NEW*. Orchestrator dispatches adversarial reviewer (separate persona) to find weaknesses BEFORE execution. Operator-relevant for high-stakes decisions.
861:17. **Think-in-code pattern** — *r48 NEW*. Represent reasoning as executable code rather than free-text deliberation. Sibling had documented BEFORE Anthropic published code-exec-with-MCP — operator foresight ratified by Anthropic.
863:18. **Task-lifecycle pattern** — *r48 NEW*. Explicit lifecycle phases for high-volume subagent dispatch — spawn → context-load → execute → review → result-merge → cleanup. Operator runs 4,680/week intuitively.
865:### §7.1 — 8 design rules for skill / agent authoring (r38 V5 SOTA kit inspiration)
872:4. **Cite-anchored** — every load-bearing claim cites primary source URL or local file:line

380: 
381: ### Per-layer rationale
382: 
383: #### L0 — SUBSTRATE: MCP everywhere
384: 
385: **What:** Universal substrate for tool/resource exposure to LLM agents. Operator runs 12 MCP servers covering github / context7 / deepwiki / playwright / chrome-devtools / repomix / serena / ccusage / gitnexus / memory / phoenix / graphiti.
386: 
387: **Operator state:** ✓ INSTALLED at correct shape.
388: 
389: **Convergence evidence (9/17 axes):** r1+r3+r7+r10+r14+r15+r17+r23+r33. **Strongest single signal of entire study** — 8/10 production orgs use MCP per r7. AAIF Linux Foundation governance Dec 9 2025.
390: 
391: **MCP spec 2025-11-25 current state (r36):**
392: - 9 major SEPs including MCP Tasks (SEP-1686 durable requests) / sampling + tool-calling / OIDC Discovery 1.0 / tool icons / JSON Schema 2020-12 default
393: - **OAuth 2.1 PKCE NOW MANDATORY** for remote MCP — S256 challenge method REQUIRED
394: - **Streamable HTTP is THE long-term transport** — HTTP+SSE deprecated March 2025
395: - 17+ reference servers ARCHIVED (Postgres / SQLite / Slack / GitHub / GitLab / Google Drive); 7 maintained: Everything / Fetch / Filesystem / Git / Memory / Sequential Thinking / Time
396: - Registry v0.1 API freeze Oct 24 2025; v1.7.9 May 12 2026 (PREVIEW, not GA)
397: - AAIF: 170+ members in 4 months; MCP at 110M+ monthly downloads; A2A v1.0 GA April 2026
398: 
399: **Anthropic Feb 17 2026 GA: Tool search tool (r33):**
400: > Structural fix for "12-MCP context flood" via dynamic tool loading from large catalogs.
401: >
402: > **codex v3 audit P1 caveat:** Tool search tool works when **the application controls the Messages API tool catalog / `defer_loading`**. It is **NOT automatically a Claude Code MCP/plugin-context switch**. Scope to: API / Managed-Agent harnesses + MCP connector/catalog flows. **Claude Code local MCP support must be verified separately** before claiming it replaces operator's 12-MCP context burden.
403: 
404: **OpenAI Agents SDK `include_server_in_tool_names` (r35):** v0.16.0 May 7 2026 — second canonical MCP-context-flood fix. Both vendors converged on namespace-based solutions.
405: 
406: #### L0.5 — SECURITY / PROVENANCE / PERMISSION (NEW per codex audit P0)
407: 
408: **What:** First-class security layer between substrate and application — addresses path allowlists / secrets boundaries / MCP source provenance / sandbox requirements.
409: 
410: **Operator state:** PARTIAL — current `bypassPermissions: true` blanket + denylist of `.env*` / `secrets/**` / `id_rsa*` / `*.pem` / `*.pfx` / `*.key`. NEEDS expansion per r37 specialist audit.
411: 
412: **11 required disciplines (per r37 + codex audit + r33):**
413: 
414: 1. **Path policy — REWRITE:** Replace `bypassPermissions: true` with **explicit allowlist + Claude Code auto mode default-deny** (Mar 25 2026 Anthropic-OFFICIAL per r33). Filesystem MCP restricted to `Z:/<project>/`; Bash command allowlist narrows to specific orgs.
415: 
416: 2. **Secrets boundary — EXPANDED denylist (r37 §2 — 12+ classes missing):**
417:    - `Read(./.aws/**)` / `Read(./.azure/**)` / `Read(./.gcloud/**)` / `Read(./.kube/**)` / `Read(./.ssh/**)`
418:    - `Read(./.docker/config.json)` / `Read(./.npmrc)` / `Read(./.git-credentials)`
419:    - `Read(./.codex/**)` / `Read(./*.kdbx)` / `Read(./*.tfstate)`
420:    - PSReadLine history: `$env:USERPROFILE\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\PowerShell\PSReadLine\ConsoleHost_history.txt`
421:    - Browser cookies / Anthropic + OpenAI key dirs
422: 
423: 3. **MCP server source verification — CODIFY as CARDINAL-RULE #6:**
424:    - Probe official MCP Registry (`github.com/modelcontextprotocol/registry`) FIRST
425:    - Cross-check Smithery.ai
426:    - Verify maintainership badge + last-commit < 90 days + LICENSE file before any install
427:    - **Verify PyPI/npm if repo archived** — r30 §2 lesson: semgrep-mcp's GitHub archived but PyPI alive
428:    - No cryptographic server signing yet (r36 gap) — rely on Registry verification
429: 
430: 4. **OAuth 2.1 PKCE — MANDATORY for remote MCP (r36):** MCP spec 2025-11-25 requires OAuth 2.1 PKCE with S256 challenge.
431: 
432: 5. **Update policy:** Pin MCP server versions in `.mcp.json` (operator already does this); review changelog before bump; never `@latest` for L0 substrate.
433: 
434: 6. **Sandbox requirements:** Tool search tool preferred over code-execution-with-MCP. If code-execution required: path/data policy + resource limits + monitoring + WSL2 boundary OR Windows Sandbox container.
435: 
436: 7. **Claude Code auto mode (Mar 25 2026 NEW r33):** Replaces blanket `bypassPermissions:true` with scoped invocation. Availability: Max/Team/Enterprise/API plans (NOT Pro alone). Operator's Pro+Max stack qualifies.
437: 
438: 8. **PowerShell tool risks — Windows-specific (r37 §3):**
439:    - AMSI bypass risk; PSReadLine history exfiltration; DPAPI/Credential Vault access; Transcripts; PSModulePath DLL planting
440:    - Never persist credentials in env vars; use Windows Credential Manager
441: 
442: 9. **Z:-portable cross-machine threat model (r37 §4):**

544: 1. Exact LiteLLM model IDs validated against current `docs.litellm.ai/docs/providers/`
545: 2. `api.deepseek.com/anthropic` endpoint smoke-test passes (auth, basic prompt, tool-call)

549:

552: #### T0.1 — LiteLLM 5-tier cascade
553: 
554: ```powershell
555: # Rationale: $69K/mo sustained / $830K/yr (r49) Opus-heavy spend (98.8% Opus); 
556: # target rebalance 50% Opus / 35% Sonnet / 15% Haiku saves $10-35K/mo range.
557: pip install 'litellm[proxy]'
558: # Use validated 5-tier YAML in §8 (Haiku → Sonnet → Opus → GPT-5.5 verify → DeepSeek V4 escape)
559: # Set ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://localhost:4000 in CLAUDE.local.md
560: # Reversible via env-var unset; <1min rollback

563: **Impact:** $10-35K/month range ($10K under Sonnet-only 3%→35% reshare; up to $35K full 50/35/15 mix)

825: ## §7 Patterns to Adopt (18 non-tool SOTA patterns)
826: 
827: The strongest convergence in the entire study was NOT a tool — it was a set of patterns. Adopt as operating discipline:
828: 
829: 1. **Evals-first** — *4 named-T2 + Anthropic-OFFICIAL* — strongest non-tool convergence. Hamel Husain + Eugene Yan + Chip Huyen + Ben Hylak. Anthropic Jan 9 2026 *Demystifying evals for AI agents* RATIFIES. Implementation: Phoenix runtime tracing + Promptfoo CI-gate.
830: 
831: 2. **Context engineering as first-class** — *3 T2 + Anthropic-OFFICIAL*. Karpathy + Cole Medin + Addy Osmani. CLAUDE.md + AGENTS.md + per-task fresh context (`/clear` discipline).
832: 
833: 3. **Plan/execute decoupling** — *3 T2 + Anthropic Mar 24 RATIFIES + 4 academic papers*. Chip Huyen + Cole Medin + Andrew Ng. Mirrors `iannuttall/ralph` ralph-dag origin. Adopt Anthropic Advisor tool API (Apr 9 2026) for first-class API-level pairing.
834: 
835: 4. **Partial autonomy > full autonomy** — *3 T2*. Karpathy + Addy + Jeremy Howard. Humans-in-loop on decision boundaries; autonomous within bounded tasks. Tempers L5 scaffold usage.
836: 
837: 5. **Scaffold-as-determinant** — *r5 + r8 + Anthropic eng blog*. *"Model is the ceiling, harness is the ladder."* Particula measured 42% → 78% on SWE-bench from scaffold alone (same model).
838: 
839: 6. **Workflows > agents until you need agents** — *Anthropic explicit r23 + r33 ratifies*. Start with workflows (prompt-chain / routing / parallelization / orchestrator-worker / evaluator-optimizer); escalate to agents only when needed.
840: 
841: 7. **Tool search tool over code-execution-with-MCP** — *Anthropic GA Feb 17 r33*. **SUPERSEDES** r23 "code-execution-with-MCP" as canonical Anthropic-OFFICIAL approach to API-layer context-flood mitigation. NOT a proven CC local MCP context switch — verify separately.
842: 
843: 8. **Memory-persistence > context-fill** — *r23 + r33*. Save plans/state to memory file BEFORE spawning subagents. Operator's `intelligent-compact` + 70% autocompact override aligns.
844: 
845: 9. **Simple composable > frameworks** — *r23 + r16 + r32*. Anthropic explicit. Validates r16's ARCHITECTURE-OVER-BUILT critique. r32 confirmation: Anthropic native structured outputs replace Instructor/BAML/Mirascope category.
846: 
847: 10. **Anthropic Extended Thinking dial** — *r27 + r33 GA Feb 5*. `thinking:{type:"adaptive"}` replaces manual `budget_tokens`. `interleaved-thinking-2025-05-14` beta header. First-class reasoning escalation for Opus 4.7 / Sonnet 4.6.
848: 
849: 11. **Anthropic-native structured outputs over 3rd-party** — *r32 NEW*. `output_config.format` + `strict:true` + 24h grammar cache. **PREFILL DEPRECATED April 2026** on latest models.
850: 
851: 12. **Context-resets > compaction** — *r33 NEW Anthropic Mar 24*. Clearing context entirely and starting fresh agent OUTPERFORMS in-place compaction for long-running tasks. Use `/clear` between sprint contracts; save state to memory file.
852: 
853: 13. **State-machine workflows for resumable processes** — *r38 NEW*. Distinct from DAG. Use explicit state-machine semantics when workflow needs resume-after-crash or branch-tracking-beyond-dependencies.
854: 
855: 14. **Worker-reviewer contract templates** — *r38 NEW*. Explicit contract: input-schema / output-schema / acceptance-criteria. Reduces ambiguous failures across worker-reviewer boundary.
856: 
857: 15. **Operating-rules router format** — *r38 NEW*. Document tool-selection heuristics in router file — explicit decision tree (situation, tool). Easier to audit than implicit policy embedded in prompts.
858: 
859: 16. **Adversarial self-critique — `grill-me` pattern** — *r48 NEW*. Orchestrator dispatches adversarial reviewer (separate persona) to find weaknesses BEFORE execution. Operator-relevant for high-stakes decisions.
860: 
861: 17. **Think-in-code pattern** — *r48 NEW*. Represent reasoning as executable code rather than free-text deliberation. Sibling had documented BEFORE Anthropic published code-exec-with-MCP — operator foresight ratified by Anthropic.
862: 
863: 18. **Task-lifecycle pattern** — *r48 NEW*. Explicit lifecycle phases for high-volume subagent dispatch — spawn → context-load → execute → review → result-merge → cleanup. Operator runs 4,680/week intuitively.
864: 
865: ### §7.1 — 8 design rules for skill / agent authoring (r38 V5 SOTA kit inspiration)
866: 
867: When authoring `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` or `.claude/agents/<name>.md`:
868: 
869: 1. **Single responsibility** — one skill = one verb
870: 2. **Explicit contract** — frontmatter `description:` states exact triggers; body states inputs/outputs
871: 3. **Fresh context where possible** — prefer subagent dispatch with fresh context
872: 4. **Cite-anchored** — every load-bearing claim cites primary source URL or local file:line
873: 5. **Reversible** — every edit/install/config change includes revert path
874: 6. **One-shot per fork** — sub-agents complete one directive then stop
875: 7. **No-sub-agent-spawning-in-fork** — fork-boilerplate forbids recursive dispatch
876: 8. **Verifiable acceptance** — every output has measurable success criteria
877: 
878: ---

880: ## §8 5-Tier LiteLLM Cascade Configuration (r30 §1 validated)
881: 
882: ```yaml
883: # LiteLLM 5-tier cost-aware cascade — W258r30 validated 2026-05-16
884: # CAVEATS:
885: # - claude-haiku-4-5 model string not in LiteLLM catalog at fetch — use exact dated 
886: #   string from docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/about-claude/models or wait for catalog refresh

889: # - codex CLI is NOT routed through this config (separate Path P subprocess)
890:

892:   # Tier 1 — cheapest (drafting, classification, summarization)
893:   - model_name: tier1-haiku
894:     litellm_params:
895:       model: anthropic/claude-haiku-4-5-VERIFY-EXACT-DATED-STRING
896:       api_key: os.environ/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
897:       rpm: 1000
898: 
899:   # Tier 2 — default Sonnet
900:   - model_name: tier2-sonnet
901:     litellm_params:
902:       model: anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6
903:       api_key: os.environ/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
904:       rpm: 500
905: 
906:   # Tier 3 — Opus for hard tasks
907:   - model_name: tier3-opus
908:     litellm_params:
909:       model: anthropic/claude-opus-4-6-20260205
910:       api_key: os.environ/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
911:       rpm: 100
912: 
913:   # Tier 4 — GPT-5.5 for cross-provider verification
914:   - model_name: tier4-gpt55
915:     litellm_params:
916:       model: openai/gpt-5.5
917:       api_key: os.environ/OPENAI_API_KEY
918:       reasoning_effort: high
919:       rpm: 100
920: 
921:   # Tier 4.5 — GPT-5.5 Instant (NEW per r31, May 5 2026 — new ChatGPT default)
922:   - model_name: tier4-gpt55-instant
923:     litellm_params:
924:       model: openai/gpt-5.5-instant
925:       api_key: os.environ/OPENAI_API_KEY
926:       rpm: 200
927: 
928:   # Tier 5 — DeepSeek cheap escape valve (NOT for multimodal/MCP-native paths)
929:   - model_name: tier5-deepseek
930:     litellm_params:
931:       model: deepseek/deepseek-chat
932:       api_key: os.environ/DEEPSEEK_API_KEY
933:       rpm: 200
934: 
935: litellm_settings:
936:   # Cascade: Haiku → Sonnet → Opus → GPT-5.5 → DeepSeek (NEVER fall back to DS for multimodal)
937:   fallbacks:
938:     - tier1-haiku: [tier2-sonnet]
939:     - tier2-sonnet: [tier3-opus]
940:     - tier3-opus: [tier4-gpt55]
940:     - tier3-opus: [tier4-gpt55]
941:     - tier4-gpt55: [tier5-deepseek]
942:   num_retries: 2
943:   context_window_fallbacks:
944:     - tier1-haiku: [tier2-sonnet, tier3-opus]   # 1M ceiling
945: 
946: router_settings:
947:   routing_strategy: usage-based-routing
948:   model_group_alias:
949:     sota-default: tier2-sonnet
950:     sota-deep: tier3-opus
951:     sota-cheap: tier1-haiku
952:     sota-verify: tier4-gpt55
953:     sota-instant: tier4-gpt55-instant
954:     sota-escape: tier5-deepseek
955:   num_retries: 2
956:   timeout: 60
957: ```

Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:1:# W258r9 — Multi-Agent Orchestration Patterns Deep-Dive (2026-05-16)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:3:**Method:** parallel-fetch 16 canonical orchestration-pattern sources via ctx_fetch_and_index (14 OK / 2× HTTP 404 = goose-docs + latent.space). Cross-checked via ctx_search 12 queries. Cite-anchors: Anthropic Engineering "Building Effective Agents" (2024-12-19) + Microsoft Magentic-One MSR article + OpenAI Swarm README + LangGraph multi-agent docs + CrewAI processes + Archon README + Eugene Yan LLM patterns + Chip Huyen "Agents" (2025-01-07) + LiteLLM routing + Anthropic Computer Use docs + OpenHands README + Simon Willison Dec-2025 + AutoGen README + DeepLearning.AI Batch.
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:7:## §1 Pattern catalog (13+ patterns)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:9:### P1 — Single agent + loop (ReAct family)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:10:**Def:** One LLM iterates `reason → act → observe → reflect` until termination. Foundation pattern from Yao et al. 2022 (ReAct paper). 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:16:### P2 — Supervisor-worker / hierarchical
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:17:**Def:** One supervisor LLM decomposes + delegates to specialized worker agents; supervisor collects results and decides next step. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:18:**Impl:** **Magentic-One** (Orchestrator + WebSurfer + FileSurfer + Coder + ComputerTerminal), CrewAI hierarchical (`manager_llm`), LangGraph supervisor topology, Anthropic SDK `Agents-as-tools`. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:20:**Strength:** matches human team mental model; clean delegation. **Limit:** supervisor becomes bottleneck + token-hog. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:23:### P3 — Swarm / peer-mesh
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:24:**Def:** Agents transfer control to each other via routines/handoffs; no central supervisor. **DEPRECATED — OpenAI Swarm replaced by Agents SDK** (per swarm README). 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:26:**Prod:** rare in 2026; Block goose has handoff-like patterns. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:30:### P4 — Pipeline / sequential handoff
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:35:**T2 advocate:** Eugene Yan (production-pattern stable).
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:37:### P5 — DAG / workflow engine
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:40:**Prod:** incident.io 12-parallel-reviewer (DAG fanout), Stripe Minions internal DAGs. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:44:### P6 — Blackboard / shared-state
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:45:**Def:** Agents read/write a shared knowledge store; coordination via convergent state. Classical MAS pattern (Hayes-Roth 1985). 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:51:### P7 — Market mechanism / auction (research only)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:55:### P8 — MoE / expert routing
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:56:**Def:** Router classifies request → routes to specialist model/agent. Distinct from supervisor: the *router doesn't synthesize*. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:62:### P9 — Parallel-N-reviewers / consensus
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:69:### P10 — Plan-execute decoupled
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:70:**Def:** Separate *planner* (long-horizon, expensive model, slow) from *executor* (short-horizon, cheap, fast). Re-plan only on stall. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:71:**Impl:** **Devin V3** (Cognition), Archon `ralph-dag` (plan stage + execute stories), OpenHands two-phase, Anthropic SDK planner-tool pattern. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:76:### P11 — Self-healing eval-loop
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:83:### P12 — Computer-use / GUI-agent loop
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:88:**T2 advocate:** Anthropic-OFFICIAL pattern; Geoffrey Litt.
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:90:### P13 — Agent-team with shared memory
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:92:**Impl:** CrewAI + mem0, Anthropic team patterns, LangGraph with checkpointer, Letta (formerly MemGPT). 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:97:### P14 (NEW) — Stall-detection + replan ★
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:98:**Def:** Magentic-One's killer innovation: **outer-loop Task Ledger** (facts/guesses/plan) + **inner-loop Progress Ledger** (per-agent assignment + completion test) + **stall-counter** — if `stall_count > 2`, kick back to outer loop to refresh facts and replan. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:101:**Strength:** unblocks dead-end paths; matches human meta-cognition. **Limit:** depends on stall-detector accuracy. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:104:### P15 (NEW) — Agents-as-tools (composable hierarchy) ★
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:111:### P16 (NEW) — Task-ledger separation ★

Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:17:**Def:** One supervisor LLM decomposes + delegates to specialized worker agents; supervisor collects results and decides next step. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:18:**Impl:** **Magentic-One** (Orchestrator + WebSurfer + FileSurfer + Coder + ComputerTerminal), CrewAI hierarchical (`manager_llm`), LangGraph supervisor topology, Anthropic SDK `Agents-as-tools`. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:20:**Strength:** matches human team mental model; clean delegation. **Limit:** supervisor becomes bottleneck + token-hog. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:23:### P3 — Swarm / peer-mesh
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:24:**Def:** Agents transfer control to each other via routines/handoffs; no central supervisor. **DEPRECATED — OpenAI Swarm replaced by Agents SDK** (per swarm README). 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:26:**Prod:** rare in 2026; Block goose has handoff-like patterns. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:30:### P4 — Pipeline / sequential handoff
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:35:**T2 advocate:** Eugene Yan (production-pattern stable).
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:37:### P5 — DAG / workflow engine
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:40:**Prod:** incident.io 12-parallel-reviewer (DAG fanout), Stripe Minions internal DAGs. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:44:### P6 — Blackboard / shared-state
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:45:**Def:** Agents read/write a shared knowledge store; coordination via convergent state. Classical MAS pattern (Hayes-Roth 1985). 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:51:### P7 — Market mechanism / auction (research only)
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:55:### P8 — MoE / expert routing
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:56:**Def:** Router classifies request → routes to specialist model/agent. Distinct from supervisor: the *router doesn't synthesize*. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:62:### P9 — Parallel-N-reviewers / consensus
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:69:### P10 — Plan-execute decoupled
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:70:**Def:** Separate *planner* (long-horizon, expensive model, slow) from *executor* (short-horizon, cheap, fast). Re-plan only on stall. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:71:**Impl:** **Devin V3** (Cognition), Archon `ralph-dag` (plan stage + execute stories), OpenHands two-phase, Anthropic SDK planner-tool pattern. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:76:### P11 — Self-healing eval-loop

Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:97:### P14 (NEW) — Stall-detection + replan ★
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:98:**Def:** Magentic-One's killer innovation: **outer-loop Task Ledger** (facts/guesses/plan) + **inner-loop Progress Ledger** (per-agent assignment + completion test) + **stall-counter** — if `stall_count > 2`, kick back to outer loop to refresh facts and replan. 
Z:\claude-sota-installed\docs\architecture\W258-multi-axis-convergence-2026-05-16\W258r9_orchestration_patterns.md:101:**Strength:** unblocks dead-end paths; matches human meta-cognition. **Limit:** depends on stall-detector accuracy.
